129	/-- Build the callee's entry state (D7/D8): check stack overflow against

134	
135	    **P1 — the frame-padding oracle** (RESUME-PROGSIM §2): `frameReg` (the only
136	    IL-observable frame base) sits at `spCaller − frameSize`, its position
137	    UNCHANGED by `pad`; the propagated `sp` drops an extra `pad` bytes — the
138	    hole the IL skips over so that, instantiating `pad f := userOff f` at
139	    `compile_sim`, IL `sp` coincides with the machine `x2` at every call depth
140	    (the `[ra][slots]` overhead the compiler adds below each user frame). The
141	    overflow check accounts for the hole too, subsuming the stack budget.
142	    `pad = fun _ => 0` reproduces the pre-P1 semantics exactly. -/
143	def frameEnter (stackLo : Word) (fd : FunDef) (pad : Nat) (argVals : List Word)
144	    (mem : Word → Byte) (spCaller : Word) : Option St :=
145	  if spCaller.toNat < stackLo.toNat + fd.frameSize + pad then none
146	  else
147	    let frameBase := spCaller - BitVec.ofNat 64 fd.frameSize
148	    let newSp := frameBase - BitVec.ofNat 64 pad
149	    let withParams : Reg → Word :=

208	/-! ### Whole-program validation: prologue + body + epilogue vs the real compiler.
209	
210	    The single-body `matchesReal` above can't exercise `.call` (isolated bodies have
211	    no callee table). `matchesRealProg` runs the FROZEN `compileProgT` and checks,
212	    per function, that its resolved slice equals `prologueI ++ emitCF … ++ epilogueI`
213	    at the layout's `fnTab` position — the decidable cross-check of the whole emit
214	    surface (marshalling, the `jal RA` call site, prologue/epilogue) BEFORE any
215	    proof leans on it. -/
216	open LowIR.Compile (compileProgT)
217	
218	/-- The RESOLVED (label-free) prologue — a direct transcription of
219	    `Compile.prologue` with `.ins` unwrapped and `storeSlot` → `storeSlotI`
220	    (`Compile.SP`/`RA`/`A`/`T0` are physical registers, position-independent). -/
221	def prologueI (fd : FunDef) : List Instr :=
222	  let params := fd.params.toList
223	  [Instr.addi SP SP (BitVec.ofInt 12 (-(totalFrame fd : Int))), Instr.sd SP RA 0]
224	  ++ params.zipIdx.flatMap (fun pi => storeSlotI pi.1 (A pi.2))
225	  ++ ((List.range (maxRegF fd + 1)).filter
226	        (fun r => r != 0 && !params.contains r && r != fd.frameReg)).map
227	       (fun r => Instr.sd SP 0 (BitVec.ofNat 12 (slotOff r)))
228	  ++ (if fd.frameReg = 0 then [] else
229	        [Instr.addi T0 SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (userOff fd))] ++ storeSlotI fd.frameReg T0)
230	
231	/-- The RESOLVED epilogue: rets → `a0..`, restore ra + sp, `jalr x0 ra 0`. -/
232	def epilogueI (fd : FunDef) : List Instr :=
233	  fd.rets.toList.zipIdx.flatMap (fun ri => loadSlotI ri.1 (A ri.2))
234	  ++ [Instr.ld RA SP 0, Instr.addi SP SP (BitVec.ofNat 12 (totalFrame fd)), Instr.jalr 0 RA 0]
235	
236	def prologueSize (fd : FunDef) : Nat := (prologueI fd).length
237	def epilogueSize (fd : FunDef) : Nat := fd.rvc + 3
238	
239	/-- Per-function cross-check against the resolved compiler output. -/
240	def matchesRealProg (P : Program) (entry : Name) : Bool :=
241	  match compileProgT P entry with
242	  | none => false
243	  | some (instrs, fns, dats) =>
244	      let dpos  : Name → Nat := fun d => (dats.lookup d).getD 0
245	      let fnPos : Name → Nat := fun f => (fns.lookup f).getD 0
246	      P.env.all fun nf =>
247	        let fd := nf.2
248	        let p := fnPos nf.1
249	        let bodyPos := p + 4 * (prologueI fd).length
250	        let epiPos := bodyPos + 4 * csize fd.body
251	        let expected :=
252	          prologueI fd ++ emitCF P.data dpos fnPos [] [] epiPos bodyPos fd.body ++ epilogueI fd
253	        (instrs.drop (p / 4)).take expected.length == expected
254

340	/-- Half-open byte range `[base, base+len)` in `toNat` (no wrap — all our
341	    addresses sit far below 2⁶⁴). -/
342	def memRange (a base : Word) (len : Nat) : Prop :=
343	  base.toNat ≤ a.toNat ∧ a.toNat < base.toNat + len
344	def memRangeB (a base : Word) (len : Nat) : Bool :=
345	  base.toNat ≤ a.toNat && a.toNat < base.toNat + len
346	
347	/-- A machine-private hole: the `[ra][slots]` area an activation adds BELOW its
348	    user frame — `(base = that activation's sp, len = userOff)`. Listed per live
349	    activation by the `CallChain` ghost (Phase 5). -/
350	abbrev Hole := Word × Nat
351	
352	/-- Total loadable blob length (code + zero-pad + data segment). -/
353	def Layout.blobLen (L : Layout) : Nat := L.segStart + (dataSegment L.data).length
354	
355	/-- `MachPriv L holes a` — `a` is machine-private: inside the code+data blob, or
356	    inside some live activation's `[ra][slots]` hole. The IL never writes these;
357	    the footprint side condition forbids IL stores here. -/
358	def MachPriv (L : Layout) (holes : List Hole) (a : Word) : Prop :=
359	  memRange a L.codeBase L.blobLen ∨ ∃ h ∈ holes, memRange a h.1 h.2
360	def machPrivB (L : Layout) (holes : List Hole) (a : Word) : Bool :=
361	  memRangeB a L.codeBase L.blobLen || holes.any (fun h => memRangeB a h.1 h.2)
362	
363	/-- The whole shared stack region `[stackLo, sp0)` — used by `prog_sim`'s
364	    coarse top-level memory agreement (the harness's output buffers live OUTSIDE
365	    it, so "agree off blob and off stack" is the composable observable; the
366	    per-hole `MachPriv` is the finer invariant `StInv` carries mid-run). -/
367	def MachStack (stackLo sp0 : Word) (a : Word) : Prop :=
368	  memRange a stackLo (sp0.toNat - stackLo.toNat)
369	
370	/-- **C2 — `OffPriv L holes sp a`**: `a` is neither machine-private (blob + live
371	    holes) NOR in the free stack `[stackLo, sp)` below the current frame. This is
372	    the domain on which IL and machine memory agree (`StInv`), and the region an
373	    IL memory op must stay inside (`MemAccOff`). The free-stack carve-out is what
374	    makes the caller's invariant RESTORABLE after a call returns: the machine

265	  downstream — see the composability note in §7.
266	
267	## 2. THE design obstacle, and decision P1 (resolve before any proving)
268	
269	**Address divergence.** IL frames and machine frames live at different
270	addresses: the machine frame adds `[ra][slot per IL reg]` overhead, so after
271	one call the IL's `frameReg` value (= IL `sp`) and the machine's user-frame
272	base (= machine `sp + userOff`) differ, and diverge cumulatively with call
273	depth. Any value derived from `frameReg` (hex1's whole label table!) differs
274	between altitudes. A plain-equality simulation relation is therefore FALSE
275	for any program that touches its frame, and a CompCert-style memory
276	injection (per-value address translation) is unsound on our flat `BitVec 64`
277	words without provenance — you cannot tell a pointer from an integer. That
278	is the borrow layer's future job, not this cut's.
279	
280	**Decision P1 — the frame-padding oracle: make the addresses EQUAL by
281	∀-quantified padding, with the compiler UNCHANGED.**
282	
283	The key arithmetic fact (verify in the code: `Compile.lean` prologue,
284	`Prog.frameEnter`): the compiler already places the user frame at the TOP of
285	the machine frame, so its addresses are `[entry_sp − frameSize, entry_sp)` —
286	**identical to the IL's frame for the first activation**. Divergence is
287	purely the cumulative `[ra][slots]` overhead of ancestor activations. So:
288	extend `frameEnter` with a padding oracle `pad : Name → Nat` (a semantics
289	parameter alongside `dbase`/`stackLo`, ∀-quantifiable exactly like `sp₀`):
290	
291	```
292	frameReg := sp − frameSize                 -- frame position UNCHANGED
293	sp'      := sp − frameSize − pad f         -- the hole the IL skips over
294	```
295	
296	`compile_sim` instantiates `pad f := userOff f = 8*(maxRegF f + 2)`, and
297	then **IL `sp` = machine `x2` at every moment**, every frame coincides at
298	every depth, and the pad hole is byte-for-byte the machine's `[ra][slots]`
299	area. Also instantiate `dbase := fun d => codeBase + segStart + off d`
300	(`dataOffsetsFrom_shift` says this IS the compiler's table). Consequences:
301	
302	- Every IL-visible value (registers, frame pointers, cref pointers,
303	  anything stored) is **numerically identical** on both sides: the relation
304	  is plain equality. No injection, no provenance, no value translation.
305	- **Zero compiler/shim/QEMU changes.** The change is confined to the IL
306	  semantics, and `pad := fun _ => 0` reproduces today's behavior exactly —
307	  every existing #guard/theorem re-greens with the default.
308	- The IL's own overflow check (`sp ≥ stackLo + frameSize + pad f`) now
309	  accounts for the machine's overhead too — the separate "stack budget"
310	  hypothesis this plan previously needed **disappears**.
311	- The differential harness gains a new power: instantiating `pad = userOff`
312	  in the IL side makes frame memory comparable BYTE-FOR-BYTE between
313	  altitudes (previously only rets/data regions were comparable). Add such
314	  tests in Phase 0 — they are the executable check of the whole P1 idea.
315	- IL-level program proofs quantify over `pad` (frames stay disjoint —
316	  padding only adds separation; frame-local reasoning is frameReg-relative
317	  and unaffected). ∀-`pad` is also future-proof: a later register allocator
318	  shrinking `userOff` changes only the instantiation, not program proofs.
319	- Const-data writes stay COHERENT (same addresses both sides); the
320	  footprint side condition shrinks to: no store into the code region or
321	  into the pad holes (the holes are listed by the `CallChain` ghost, §4
322	  Phase 5).
323	
324	**Alternatives considered and rejected** (analyses from the 2026-07-02
325	design discussion — kept because the next reader will re-derive them):
326	
327	- *Two-stack lowering* (x2 = ra+slots, new x3 mirroring IL sp): achieves the
328	  same equality but by CHANGING the compiler — burns a register, adds
329	  2 i

/-- A function: declared parameter/return registers (callee-side names), a
    frame size in bytes, the register that receives the frame base, a body. -/
structure FunDef where
  argc      : Nat
  rvc       : Nat
  params    : Vector Reg argc
  rets      : Vector Reg rvc
  frameSize : Nat
  frameReg  : Reg
  body      : Stmt

abbrev Env := List (Name × FunDef)

/-- The program's read-only data segment: named constant byte objects,
    referenced from code as slices via `cref` (address) + `clen` (length). -/
abbrev Data := List (Name × List Byte)

113	            = L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * (pos / 4 + j)) := by

117	/-! ## Machine-step lemmas (one decoded instruction ⇒ one `step`). -/
118	
119	theorem step_addi (m : State) (rd rs : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
120	    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .addi rd rs imm) :
121	    step m = (m.rset rd (m.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64)).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by
122	  simp only [step, h]
123	
124	theorem step_add (m : State) (rd r1 r2 : Nat) (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .add rd r1 r2) :
125	    step m = (m.rset rd (m.rget r1 + m.rget r2)).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by simp only [step, h]
126	
127	theorem step_sub (m : State) (rd r1 r2 : Nat) (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .sub rd r1 r2) :
128	    step m = (m.rset rd (m.rget r1 - m.rget r2)).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by simp only [step, h]
129	
130	theorem step_or (m : State) (rd r1 r2 : Nat) (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .or rd r1 r2) :
131	    step m = (m.rset rd (m.rget r1 ||| m.rget r2)).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by simp only [step, h]
132	
133	theorem step_slli (m : State) (rd rs sh : Nat) (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .slli rd rs sh) :
134	    step m = (m.rset rd (m.rget rs <<< sh)).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by simp only [step, h]
135	
136	theorem step_srli (m : State) (rd rs sh : Nat) (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .srli rd rs sh) :
137	    step m = (m.rset rd (m.rget rs >>> sh)).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by simp only [step, h]
138	
139	theorem step_ld (m : State) (rd rs : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
140	    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .ld rd rs imm) :
141	    step m = (m.rset rd (m.loadWord (m.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64))).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by
142	  simp only [step, h]
143	
144	theorem step_sd (m : State) (rs1 rs2 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
145	    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .sd rs1 rs2 imm) :
146	    step m = (m.storeWord (m.rget rs1 + imm.signExtend 64) (m.rget rs2)).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by
147	  simp only [step, h]
148	
149	theorem step_lbu (m : State) (rd rs : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
150	    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .lbu rd rs imm) :
151	    step m = (m.rset rd ((m.loadByte (m.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64)).setWidth 64)).setPc
152	               (m.pc + 4) := by
153	  simp only [step, h]
154	
155	theorem step_sb (m : State) (rs1 rs2 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
156	    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .sb rs1 rs2 imm) :
157	    step m = (m.storeByte (m.rget rs1 + imm.signExtend 64) ((m.rget rs2).setWidth 8)).setPc
158	               (m.pc + 4) := by
159	  simp only [step, h]
160

162	
163	    `jal x0` (the `.jmp` lowering) writes x0 (discarded) and jumps `pc + off`; the
164	    four branches jump `pc + off` when their condition holds, else fall through to
165	    `pc + 4`. The offset is `imm.signExtend 64` with `imm` the resolved byte delta
166	    `target − here`. -/

80	def Emitted (L : Layout) (pos : Nat) (is : List Instr) : Prop :=
81	  pos % 4 = 0 ∧ ∀ (j : Nat) (h : j < is.length),
82	    ∃ (h2 : pos / 4 + j < L.instrs.length), L.instrs[pos / 4 + j] = is[j]'h
83	
84	/-- `Emitted` of a concatenation splits: `is₁` at `pos`, `is₂` at `pos + 4|is₁|`. -/
85	theorem Emitted_append_left (L : Layout) (pos : Nat) (is₁ is₂ : List Instr)
86	    (h : Emitted L pos (is₁ ++ is₂)) : Emitted L pos is₁ := by
87	  obtain ⟨ha, hf⟩ := h
88	  refine ⟨ha, fun j hj => ?_⟩
89	  obtain ⟨h2, he⟩ := hf j (by simp only [List.length_append]; omega)
90	  exact ⟨h2, by rw [he, List.getElem_append_left hj]⟩
91	
92	theorem Emitted_append_right (L : Layout) (pos : Nat) (is₁ is₂ : List Instr)
93	    (h : Emitted L pos (is₁ ++ is₂)) : Emitted L (pos + 4 * is₁.length) is₂ := by
94	  obtain ⟨ha, hf⟩ := h
95	  refine ⟨by omega, fun j hj => ?_⟩
96	  obtain ⟨h2, he⟩ := hf (is₁.length + j) (by simp only [List.length_append]; omega)
97	  have hpos : (pos + 4 * is₁.length) / 4 + j = pos / 4 + (is₁.length + j) := by omega
```

23	structure State where
24	  reg : Nat → Word          -- x0..x31; x0 hardwired to 0 by rget/rset
25	  pc  : Word
26	  mem : Word → Byte
27	
28	/-- Read register `i` (x0 reads as 0). -/
29	@[inline] def State.rget (s : State) (i : Nat) : Word :=
30	  if i = 0 then 0 else s.reg i
31	
32	/-- Write register `i` (writes to x0 are ignored). -/
33	@[inline] def State.rset (s : State) (i : Nat) (v : Word) : State :=
34	  if i = 0 then s else { s with reg := fun j => if j = i then v else s.reg j }
35	
36	@[inline] def State.setPc (s : State) (p : Word) : State := { s with pc := p }
37	
38	@[inline] def State.loadByte (s : State) (a : Word) : Byte := s.mem a
39	
40	@[inline] def State.storeByte (s : State) (a : Word) (b : Byte) : State :=
41	  { s with mem := fun x => if x = a then b else s.mem x }
42	
43	/-- Fetch the little-endian 32-bit word at `pc`. -/
44	def fetch32 (s : State) : BitVec 32 :=
45	  let b0 := (s.mem s.pc).setWidth 32
46	  let b1 := (s.mem (s.pc + 1)).setWidth 32
47	  let b2 := (s.mem (s.pc + 2)).setWidth 32
48	  let b3 := (s.mem (s.pc + 3)).setWidth 32
49	  b0 ||| (b1 <<< 8) ||| (b2 <<< 16) ||| (b3 <<< 24)
50	
51	/-- Extract `len` bits starting at bit `lo` as a Nat. -/
52	@[inline] def field (w : BitVec 32) (lo len : Nat) : Nat :=
53	  (w.toNat >>> lo) &&& (2 ^ len - 1)
54	
55	inductive Instr where
56	  | addi (rd rs1 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
57	  | add  (rd rs1 rs2 : Nat)
58	  | sub  (rd rs1 rs2 : Nat)
59	  | or   (rd rs1 rs2 : Nat)
60	  | slli (rd rs1 : Nat) (shamt : Nat)
61	  | srli (rd rs1 : Nat) (shamt : Nat)
62	  | lbu  (rd rs1 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
63	  | ld   (rd rs1 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
64	  | sb   (rs1 rs2 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
65	  | sd   (rs1 rs2 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
66	  | beq  (rs1 rs2 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 13)
67	  | blt  (rs1 rs2 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 13)

32	/-! ## Call-marshalling emit helpers (arg loads / ret stores).
33	
34	    A `call` lowers to: one `loadSlotI arg[i] (A i)` per argument (always 1
35	    instruction each, incl. reg 0 → `addi (A i) x0 0`), then `jal RA` to the
36	    callee, then one `storeSlotI ret[i] (A i)` per RETURN (0 instructions for

38	    `x10+i`), validated by `matchesRealProg` below. -/

theorem prologue_sim (L : Layout) (fd : FunDef) (holes : List Hole)
    (m : State) (sp0 ra : Word) (callee : St) (argVals : List Word) (p : Nat)
    (hpc : m.pc = L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 p)
    (hem : Emitted L p (prologueI fd))
    (hinst : Installed L m)
    (hsp0 : m.rget SP = sp0)
    (hra : m.rget RA = ra)
    (hargs : ∀ i, (hi : i < argVals.length) → m.rget (A i) = argVals[i])
    (hlen : fd.params.toList.length = argVals.length)
    (hparb : ∀ x ∈ fd.params.toList, x ≤ maxRegF fd)
    (hfrb : fd.frameReg ≤ maxRegF fd)
    (hcsp : callee.sp = sp0 - BitVec.ofNat 64 (totalFrame fd))
    (hcrg : ∀ r, 1 ≤ r → callee.rget r
              = if r = fd.frameReg then sp0 - BitVec.ofNat 64 fd.frameSize
                else parkFold (fun _ => 0) (fd.params.toList.zip argVals) r)
    (hmemF : ∀ a, OffPriv L ((callee.sp, userOff fd) :: holes) callee.sp a → callee.mem a = m.mem a)
    (htf : totalFrame fd ≤ 2000)
    (hfs8 : fd.frameSize % 8 = 0)
    (hsp0align : sp0.toNat % 8 = 0)
    (hsp0ge : totalFrame fd ≤ sp0.toNat)
    (hseg : 4 * L.instrs.length ≤ L.segStart)
    (hblob : L.codeBase.toNat + L.blobLen ≤ 2 ^ 64)
    (hbdc : L.codeBase.toNat + L.blobLen ≤ callee.sp.toNat
              ∨ callee.sp.toNat + userOff fd ≤ L.codeBase.toNat)
    (hholes_ord : ∀ h ∈ holes, sp0.toNat ≤ (h.1 : Word).toNat)
    (hholes_nw : ∀ h ∈ holes, (h.1 : Word).toNat + h.2 ≤ 2 ^ 64) :
    ∃ k, StInv L fd ((callee.sp, userOff fd) :: holes) callee (stepN k m)
       ∧ (stepN k m).pc = L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (p + 4 * prologueSize fd)
       ∧ (stepN k m).loadWord callee.sp = ra
       ∧ (∀ a : Word, ¬ memRange a callee.sp (userOff fd) → (stepN k m).mem a = m.mem a) := by

=== StInv def ===
def StInv (L : Layout) (fd : FunDef) (holes : List Hole) (s : St) (m : State) : Prop :=
  m.rget 2 = s.sp
  ∧ (∀ r, 1 ≤ r → r ≤ maxRegF fd →
        s.rget r = m.loadWord (s.sp + BitVec.ofNat 64 (slotOff r)))
  ∧ Installed L m
  ∧ (∀ a, OffPriv L holes s.sp a → s.mem a = m.mem a)
  ∧ holes.head? = some (s.sp, userOff fd)
  ∧ s.sp.toNat % 8 = 0
  ∧ (∀ h ∈ holes, s.sp.toNat ≤ h.1.toNat)          -- C2: live holes sit at-or-above sp (LIFO)
  ∧ (∀ h ∈ holes, h.1.toNat + h.2 ≤ 2 ^ 64)        -- C2: each hole is wrap-free

=== step_jalr / jalr_lands sigs ===

theorem step_addi (m : State) (rd rs : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .addi rd rs imm) :
    step m = (m.rset rd (m.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64)).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by
=== theorem step_ld ===
theorem step_ld (m : State) (rd rs : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .ld rd rs imm) :
    step m = (m.rset rd (m.loadWord (m.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64))).setPc (m.pc + 4) := by
=== theorem step_jalr ===
theorem step_jalr (m : State) (rd rs1 : Nat) (imm : BitVec 12)
    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .jalr rd rs1 imm) :

theorem jalr_lands (m : State) (rs1 : Nat) (ra : Word)
    (hd : decode (fetch32 m) = .jalr 0 rs1 (0 : BitVec 12))

=== theorem run_load ===
theorem run_load (L : Layout) (fd : FunDef) (holes : List Hole) (s : St) (m : State)
    (r t q : Nat) (hinv : StInv L fd holes s m) (hr : r ≤ maxRegF fd) (hfr : slotOff r < 2 ^ 11)
    (ht2 : t ≠ 2) (ht0 : t ≠ 0) (hq : m.pc = L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 q)
    (hem : Emitted L q (loadSlotI r t)) :
    StInv L fd holes s (step m)
    ∧ (step m).pc = L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (q + 4)
    ∧ (step m).mem = m.mem
    ∧ (step m).rget t = s.rget r
    ∧ ∀ t', t' ≠ t → (step m).rget t' = m.rget t' := by

=== Installed def ===
def Installed (L : Layout) (m : State) : Prop :=
  (∀ (j : Nat) (h : j < L.instrs.length),
      Rv64i.decode (Rv64i.fetch32 { m with pc := L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * j) })
        = L.instrs[j]'h)
  ∧ (∀ (i : Nat) (h : i < (dataSegment L.data).length),
      m.mem (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (L.segStart + i)) = (dataSegment L.data)[i]'h)

=== Installed_congr / Installed_setPc / decode_at ===
theorem Installed_setPc (L : Layout) (m : State) (p : Word) (h : Installed L m) :
    Installed L (m.setPc p) := h
theorem Installed_congr (L : Layout) (m m' : State) (hmem : m'.mem = m.mem)
    (h : Installed L m) : Installed L m' := by
--- decode_at ---
theorem decode_at (L : Layout) (m mj : State) (pos : Nat) (is : List Instr)
    (hem : Emitted L pos is) (hinst : Installed L m) (j : Nat) (hj : j < is.length)
    (hpcj : mj.pc = L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (pos + 4 * j)) (hmemj : mj.mem = m.mem) :
    decode (fetch32 mj) = is[j]'hj := by
```

### 🛠️ **Tool result**  ·  22:47:50; tool 1.3s

## 4. The call case, assembled (the six-segment ledger)

In `lower_sim_cf`, `case call argc rvc f args rets`, after `exec_call_*`
unfolders split `hexec` (lookup `some fd'`, arity true, `frameEnter = some
callee`, body result `.normal`/`.ret` — other branches contradict `hexec`):

1. **Marshal** (`4·argc` bytes): `run_marshal` — A-regs = arg values (caller
   StInv slots; arg regs ≤ `maxRegF fd` from `hreg`'s call arm), StInv
   preserved, pc at the `jal`.
2. **`jal RA δ`**: `step_jal` + `jump_lands` — pc = `codeBase + fnPos f`,
   RA = `codeBase + (here + 4·argc + 4)` (the return address), StInv via
   `StInv_scratch` (RA ≠ 2). Range: `here < 2²⁰` from `hbnd`,
   `fnPos f < 2²⁰` from `hfn`.
3. **Prologue**: `prologue_sim` — callee `StInv` at holes
   `(callee.sp, userOff fd') :: holes`, ra slot holds the return address,
   pc = `bodyPos`.
4. **Body — the fuel IH** (the inductive use of the evaluator theorem):
   apply `ih` at `fd := fd'`, `holes := (callee.sp, userOff fd') :: holes`,
   `epiPos := epiPos'`, `brkPos := []`, `contPos := []`, `here := bodyPos`,
   the callee `StInv`, `hexec` = the body run. Landing for BOTH live
   outcomes is `codeBase + epiPos'` (`.normal`: fall-through
   `bodyPos + 4·csize = epiPos'`; `.ret`: `landPos … .ret = epiPos'`) — one
   case split, same continuation. Plus `FramesPres` for the extended holes
   (C3): ra slot + all caller hole bytes intact.
5. **Epilogue**: `epilogue_sim` — machine sp back to `s.sp` (P1: structural
   restore on both sides), A-regs = ret values, pc = return address.
   Intermediate caller `StInv` for `{s with mem := s1.mem}` (regs unchanged):
   sp ✓; slots = pre-call machine bytes (`FramesPres`) = pre-call IL regs ✓;
   `Installed` threaded ✓; memory agreement — for `a ∉ MachPriv holes` and
   `a ≥ s.sp`: `a` is outside the callee hole (below sp) and above
   `callee.sp`, so the callee-exit agreement covers it ✓ (this is where C2
   pays); C2's ordering/no-wrap conjuncts: restore trivially.
6. **Ret-stores** (`4·#{nonzero rets}` bytes): `run_retstores` — the IL fold
   and the machine stores update in lockstep; final pc =
   `codeBase + here + 4·csize(call)` = `landPos … .normal` ✓; outcome is
   `.normal` ✓; `FramesPres` for the CALLER's holes: segments 1–2 write no
   memory, 3–5's writes are in the callee frame (disjoint from caller holes
   by the C2 ordering conjunct), 6 writes the caller's own slot window —
   exactly the exclusion ✓.

### Side-condition propagation to the callee's IH instance

| Hypothesis | Source |
|---|---|
| `hreg` | free: `maxRegS fd'.body ≤ maxRegF fd'` by `maxRegF`'s definition |
| `hnw` | `sp' + userOff' ≤ sp' + totalFrame' = s.sp.toNat ≤ 2⁶⁴` (frameEnter no-wrap + `hpad`) |

135	    **P1 — the frame-padding oracle** (RESUME-PROGSIM §2): `frameReg` (the only
136	    IL-observable frame base) sits at `spCaller − frameSize`, its position
137	    UNCHANGED by `pad`; the propagated `sp` drops an extra `pad` bytes — the
138	    hole the IL skips over so that, instantiating `pad f := userOff f` at
139	    `compile_sim`, IL `sp` coincides with the machine `x2` at every call depth
140	    (the `[ra][slots]` overhead the compiler adds below each user frame). The
141	    overflow check accounts for the hole too, subsuming the stack budget.
142	    `pad = fun _ => 0` reproduces the pre-P1 semantics exactly. -/
143	def frameEnter (stackLo : Word) (fd : FunDef) (pad : Nat) (argVals : List Word)
144	    (mem : Word → Byte) (spCaller : Word) : Option St :=
145	  if spCaller.toNat < stackLo.toNat + fd.frameSize + pad then none
146	  else
147	    let frameBase := spCaller - BitVec.ofNat 64 fd.frameSize
148	    let newSp := frameBase - BitVec.ofNat 64 pad
149	    let withParams : Reg → Word :=
150	      (fd.params.toList.zip argVals).foldl
151	        (fun rf pv => fun r => if r = pv.1 then pv.2 else rf r) (fun _ => 0)
152	    some { regs := fun r => if r = fd.frameReg then frameBase else withParams r
153	           mem  := mem
154	           sp   := newSp }

423	  spAligned    : sp0.toNat % 8 = 0
424	  stackNonEmpty : stackLo.toNat ≤ sp0.toNat
425	  blobStackDisjoint : ∀ a, memRange a L.codeBase L.blobLen → ¬ MachStack stackLo sp0 a

427	/-- The padding oracle `compile_sim` instantiates: `userOff` of each function.
428	    With this, IL `sp` ≡ machine `x2` at every depth (validated in
429	    `CompileTests.p1_*`). -/
430	def userPad (env : Env) : Name → Nat := fun f => (List.lookup f env).elim 0 userOff
431	
432	/-! ## §3.3 — the program-level payoff `prog_sim` (statement; proof deferred).
433	
434	    `lower_sim`/`call_sim` (§3.2/§3.3) are the induction's workhorses; their
435	    statements need the compile-time `Emitted` predicate (byte-stream ↔ lowering
436	    + resolved label addresses) that Phase 2 (AsmFacts) characterizes and the
437	    Phase 4.1 VERTICAL SLICE validates against the differential oracle — the
438	    go/no-go checkpoint for this whole relation. They land there, not guessed
439	    here. `prog_sim` below is self-contained (no `Emitted`) and is the corollary
440	    every ProgLib function composes with. -/
441	
442	/-- **`prog_sim`** — if the D7/D8 IL says `entry(args)` computes `s'` (with the
443	    P1 padding `userPad`), then the compiled RV64I blob, started at `codeBase`
444	    with `args` in `a0..` and `sp = sp0`, runs to the halt pad in a state whose
445	    `a0..` hold `entry`'s return values and whose memory agrees with `s'`
446	    everywhere outside the blob and the stack. Const data is placed at
447	    `codeBase + segStart` on BOTH sides (`dataOffsetsFrom_shift`). -/
448	theorem prog_sim
449	    {P : Program} {entry : Name} {fd : FunDef} {args : List Word}
450	    {stackLo sp0 : Word} {fuel : Nat} {s' : St} {L : Layout} {m0 : State}

452	    (hL     : layoutOf P entry L.codeBase L.stackLo = some L)
453	    (hpre   : SimPre L stackLo sp0)
454	    (hpc    : m0.pc = L.codeBase)
455	    (hsp    : m0.rget 2 = sp0)
456	    (hargs  : ∀ i, i < fd.argc → m0.rget (10 + i) = args.getD i 0)
457	    (hinst  : Installed L m0)
458	    (hmem   : ∀ a, ¬ MachPriv L [] a →
459	                installData (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 L.segStart) P.data (fun _ => 0) a

59	/-- Build the `Layout` for `entry` at `codeBase` straight from the FROZEN
60	    compiler. `none` iff the program is uncompilable (same condition as
61	    `compileProgT`). `segStart` is recomputed here from the resolved stream
62	    and is DEFINITIONALLY the compiler's `pad8 codeEnd` (proved in Phase 2). -/
63	def layoutOf (P : Program) (entry : Name) (codeBase stackLo : Word) : Option Layout :=
64	  match compileProgT P entry with
65	  | some (is, fns, _) =>
66	      some { codeBase, instrs := is, fnTab := fns,
67	             segStart := pad8 (4 * is.length), data := P.data, stackLo }
68	  | none => none
69	
70	/-- The machine halt pc: the self-loop landing pad the compiler puts at
71	    `codeBase+4` (the entry stub is `jal ra,entry` at `codeBase`; the entry
72	    returns to `codeBase+4`). Run the machine with `runFuel L.haltPc`. -/
73	def Layout.haltPc (L : Layout) : Word := L.codeBase + 4
74	
75	/-- Address one past the last instruction — the code window's top. -/
76	def Layout.codeEnd (L : Layout) : Word :=
77	  L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * L.instrs.length)
78	
79	/-- `Installed L m` — the compiled blob is faithfully present in `m`'s memory:
80	    (code) the 32-bit word at `codeBase + 4j` decodes via the TRUSTED
81	    `Rv64i.decode` to `instrs[j]`; (data) the const-data byte at
82	    `codeBase + segStart + i` equals `dataSegment data`'s byte `i`
83	    (`Prog.dataSegment_at` already relates that segment to the object bytes). -/
84	def Installed (L : Layout) (m : State) : Prop :=
85	  (∀ (j : Nat) (h : j < L.instrs.length),

/-! ## Executable sanity (`#guard`) — the def oracle (RESUME-PROGSIM §6). -/

section Guards
open LowIR.Prog (chainEnv sumData frameLocal sub3 caller STACK_LO SP0 zeroMem)

/-- A bare machine with `blob` loaded at `CB`, everything else zero. -/
private def CB : Word := 0x10000
private def mkM (blob : List Byte) : State :=
  { reg := fun _ => 0, pc := CB, mem := LowIR.loadMem CB blob 0x20000 [] }

-- CODE half of `Installed`: the compiled chain decodes back, byte-for-byte.
#guard (do let blob ← progBytes chainEnv "f3"
           let L ← layoutOf chainEnv "f3" CB 0x4000
           pure (codeInstalledB L (mkM blob))).getD false = true

-- CODE + DATA halves: `sumData` has a const-data object in the blob.
#guard (do let blob ← progBytes sumData "sumd"
           let L ← layoutOf sumData "sumd" CB 0
           pure (codeInstalledB L (mkM blob) && dataInstalledB L (mkM blob))).getD false = true

-- `execT` erases to `exec`: same observable return on the fixtures.
#guard (runT [("sub3", sub3)] STACK_LO 1000 "sub3" [30, 12, 2] zeroMem SP0).map
        (fun soo => (soo.1.rget 10).toNat) = some 40
#guard (runT [("frameLocal", frameLocal)] STACK_LO 1000 "frameLocal" [0xDEAD] zeroMem SP0).map
        (fun soo => (soo.1.rget 10).toNat) = some 0xDEAD

-- FOOTPRINTS: sub3 writes no memory; frameLocal's `sd` writes exactly 8 bytes;
-- caller inherits the callee `frameLocal`'s 8-byte footprint through the call.
#guard (runT [("sub3", sub3)] STACK_LO 1000 "sub3" [30, 12, 2] zeroMem SP0).map
        (fun soo => soo.2.2.length) = some 0
#guard (runT [("frameLocal", frameLocal)] STACK_LO 1000 "frameLocal" [0xDEAD] zeroMem SP0).map

131:    (h : exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel a s = some (s', .ret)) :
132:    exec P dbase pad stackLo (fuel+1) (.seq a b) s = some (s', .ret) := by simp [exec, h]

157:    (h : exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel body s = some (s', .ret)) :
158:    exec P dbase pad stackLo (fuel+1) (.block body) s = some (s', .ret) := by simp [exec, h]

209:theorem exec_call_ok (fuel argc rvc : Nat) (f : Name) (args : Vector Reg argc)

216:    exec P dbase pad stackLo (fuel+1) (.call argc rvc f args rets) s
217:      = some ((rets.toList.zip (fd.rets.toList.map s1.rget)).foldl
221:theorem exec_call_lookup_none (fuel argc rvc : Nat) (f : Name) (args : Vector Reg argc)
222:    (rets : Vector Reg rvc) (s : St) (hlk : List.lookup f P.env = none) :
223:    exec P dbase pad stackLo (fuel+1) (.call argc rvc f args rets) s = none := by simp [exec, hlk]

def maxRegF (fd : FunDef) : Nat :=
  max (maxRegS fd.body) <|
  max fd.frameReg <|
  max (fd.params.toList.foldl max 0) (fd.rets.toList.foldl max 0)

/-- Byte offset of the user frame within the machine frame. -/
def userOff (fd : FunDef) : Nat := 8 * (maxRegF fd + 2)

/-- Total machine frame size. -/
def totalFrame (fd : FunDef) : Nat := userOff fd + fd.frameSize

=== theorem step_jal  ===
theorem step_jal (m : State) (rd : Nat) (imm : BitVec 21)
    (h : decode (fetch32 m) = .jal rd imm) :
    step m = (m.rset rd (m.pc + 4)).setPc (m.pc + imm.signExtend 64) := by simp only [step, h]
=== theorem jump_lands ===
theorem jump_lands (cb : Word) (here tgt : Nat) :
    cb + BitVec.ofNat 64 here + BitVec.ofInt 64 ((tgt : Int) - (here : Int))
      = cb + BitVec.ofNat 64 tgt := by
=== theorem StInv_scratch ===
theorem StInv_scratch (L : Layout) (fd : FunDef) (holes : List Hole) (s : St)
    (m : State) (a : Nat) (v p : Word) (ha : a ≠ 2) (hinv : StInv L fd holes s m) :
    StInv L fd holes s ((m.rset a v).setPc p) := by
=== theorem run_retStoresFrom ===
theorem run_retStoresFrom (L : Layout) (fd : FunDef) (holes : List Hole)
    (hnw : ∀ s : St, s.sp.toNat + userOff fd ≤ 2 ^ 64)
    (hseg : 4 * L.instrs.length ≤ L.segStart)
    (hblob : L.codeBase.toNat + L.blobLen ≤ 2 ^ 64) :
    ∀ (rets : List Nat) (vs : List Word) (base q : Nat) (s : St) (m : State),
      StInv L fd holes s m →
      m.pc = L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 q →
      Emitted L q ((rets.zipIdx base).flatMap fun ri => storeSlotI ri.1 (A ri.2)) →
      (∀ r ∈ rets, r ≤ maxRegF fd) →
      (∀ r ∈ rets, slotOff r < 2 ^ 11) →
      (L.codeBase.toNat + L.blobLen ≤ s.sp.toNat
         ∨ s.sp.toNat + userOff fd ≤ L.codeBase.toNat) →
      rets.length = vs.length →
      (∀ i, (hi : i < vs.length) → m.rget (A (base + i)) = vs[i]) →
      ∃ k, StInv L fd holes ((rets.zip vs).foldl (fun st rv => st.rset rv.1 rv.2) s) (stepN k m)
         ∧ (stepN k m).pc = L.codeBase
             + BitVec.ofNat 64 (q + 4 * ((rets.zipIdx base).flatMap
                 fun ri => storeSlotI ri.1 (A ri.2)).length)
=== def maxRegS ===
def maxRegS : PStmt → Nat
  | .skip | .annot _ | .ret | .brkB _ | .contL _ => 0
  | .seq a b          => max (maxRegS a) (maxRegS b)
  | .addi rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .add  rd r1 r2    => max rd (max r1 r2)
  | .sub  rd r1 r2    => max rd (max r1 r2)
  | .orr  rd r1 r2    => max rd (max r1 r2)
  | .slli rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .srli rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .lbu  rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .ld   rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .sb   rb rv _     => max rb rv
  | .sd   rb rv _     => max rb rv
  | .ife _ a b t e    => max (max a b) (max (maxRegS t) (maxRegS e))
  | .while _ a b body => max (max a b) (maxRegS body)
  | .block body       => maxRegS body
  | .call _ _ _ args rets =>
      max (args.toList.foldl max 0) (rets.toList.foldl max 0)
=== def csize ===
def csize : PStmt → Nat
  | .seq a b        => csize a + csize b
  | .ret            => 1
  | .brkB _         => 1
  | .contL _        => 1
  | .block body     => csize body
  | .ife _ _ _ t e  => 4 + csize e + csize t
  | .while _ _ _ b  => 5 + csize b
  | .clen rd _      => 3 + (if rd = 0 then 0 else 1)   -- synthConst (3) + storeSlot
  | .cref rd _      => 5 + (if rd = 0 then 0 else 1)   -- pc-read + synth + add + store
  | .call _ _ _ args rets =>                           -- argc loads + jal + ret stores
      args.toList.length + 1 + (retStoresI rets.toList).length
  | s               => (emit s).length

/-- The label-aware lowering. `brkPos`/`contPos` are the enclosing block-end /
    loop-top label byte positions (indexed like `brkB`/`contL`'s de-Bruijn
    indices); `epiPos` the epilogue; `here` the byte offset of this code.
    Straight-line cases reuse `emit` (`here` irrelevant). -/
```

=== FunDef ===
structure FunDef where
  argc      : Nat
  rvc       : Nat
  params    : Vector Reg argc
  rets      : Vector Reg rvc
  frameSize : Nat
  frameReg  : Reg
  body      : Stmt

abbrev Env := List (Name × FunDef)

/-- The program's read-only data segment: named constant byte objects,
    referenced from code as slices via `cref` (address) + `clen` (length). -/
abbrev Data := List (Name × List Byte)

/-- A whole program: functions plus const data. Where the data objects LIVE
    is not part of the program — the semantics takes a base map (`dbase`),
    ∀-quantifiable like D8's `sp₀`, so programs stay address-independent. -/
structure Program where
  env  : Env
  data : Data

=== St + rget ===

structure St where
  regs : Reg → Word
  mem  : Word → Byte
  sp   : Word

@[inline] def St.rget (s : St) (i : Reg) : Word := if i = 0 then 0 else s.regs i

342:def memRange (a base : Word) (len : Nat) : Prop :=

===
def memRange (a base : Word) (len : Nat) : Prop :=
  base.toNat ≤ a.toNat ∧ a.toNat < base.toNat + len
def memRangeB (a base : Word) (len : Nat) : Bool :=
  base.toNat ≤ a.toNat && a.toNat < base.toNat + len

def MachPriv (L : Layout) (holes : List Hole) (a : Word) : Prop :=
  memRange a L.codeBase L.blobLen ∨ ∃ h ∈ holes, memRange a h.1 h.2
def machPrivB (L : Layout) (holes : List Hole) (a : Word) : Bool :=
  memRangeB a L.codeBase L.blobLen || holes.any (fun h => memRangeB a h.1 h.2)

def MachStack (stackLo sp0 : Word) (a : Word) : Prop :=
  memRange a stackLo (sp0.toNat - stackLo.toNat)

def OffPriv (L : Layout) (holes : List Hole) (sp a : Word) : Prop :=
  ¬ MachPriv L holes a ∧ ¬ memRange a L.stackLo (sp.toNat - L.stackLo.toNat)
```

Both `prologue_sim` and `epilogue_sim` verified axiom-clean.

## Remaining: call-assembly segments 3–6

I hit the genuine crux and stopped rather than guess. **The blocker is `prologue_sim`'s `hmemF`** (and the same in seg 5): it reduces to `s.mem a = m.mem a` on `OffPriv_callee a`, but `OffPriv_callee ⇏ OffPriv_caller` — the user frame `[s.sp−frameSize, s.sp)` (frameReg locals, IL-visible so not a hole) is in the callee's `OffPriv` yet sits inside the free stack `[stackLo, s.sp)` that the caller's `StInv` c4 deliberately excludes. Discharging it needs the **free-stack initial-agreement invariant** (both sides start equal at `installData`, neither writes below `sp` within a frame) or a `StInv` strengthening — this is exactly the "where C2 pays" subtlety, and it warrants studying `RESUME-PROGSIM` §2 before writing code.

Everything else in segs 3–6 is plumbing — I've itemised each atom application and every side-condition's source in **`docs/RESUME-CALL.md` §6**, and updated the campaign memory. The next session can resume directly from there.
